Welcome to GitHub Agentic Workflows = Actions + Agent + Safety.\nHere are some pointers to get you started in using this tool.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCreate a new workflow: \u003ca href=\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/create.md\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehttps://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/create.md\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eInstall: \u003ca href=\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/install.md\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehttps://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/install.md\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePackage a workflow repository: \u003ca href=\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/package.md\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehttps://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/package.md\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eReference: \u003ca href=\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/.github/aw/github-agentic-workflows.md\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehttps://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/.github/aw/github-agentic-workflows.md\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eGitHub Agentic Workflows (\u003ccode\u003egh-aw\u003c/code\u003e) lets developers define AI-powered repository automation in Markdown with YAML frontmatter and run AI agents securely through GitHub Actions. The \u003ccode\u003egh-aw\u003c/code\u003e GitHub CLI extension compiles each agentic workflow into a standard GitHub Actions workflow.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eUse conventional GitHub Actions for deterministic builds, tests, linting, deployments, and reproducible scripts. Add an agentic workflow when a task needs reasoning or interpretation, such as issue triage, pull-request review, CI failure investigation, documentation maintenance, dependency analysis, or repository reporting. GitHub Agentic Workflows complements existing CI/CD; it does not replace it.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBuilt-in AI engines include GitHub Copilot, Claude Code, OpenAI Codex, Google Gemini, and Pi. Agent jobs are read-only and sandboxed by default, and configured GitHub writes are normally applied through validated \u003ccode\u003esafe-outputs\u003c/code\u003e jobs with scoped permissions.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eContents\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"gh extension install github/gh-aw\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003egh extension install github/gh-aw\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThen follow the \u003ca href=\"https://github.github.com/gh-aw/setup/quick-start/\"\u003eGitHub Agentic Workflows quickstart\u003c/a\u003e to select an AI engine, add a sample workflow, and run it through GitHub Actions.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eHow Agentic Workflows work\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-how-agentic-workflows-work\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: How Agentic Workflows work\" href=\"#how-agentic-workflows-work\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAn agentic workflow has two parts: YAML frontmatter configures triggers, permissions, tools, and the AI engine; the Markdown body tells the AI agent what to accomplish. The \u003ccode\u003egh aw compile\u003c/code\u003e command validates this source and generates the \u003ccode\u003e.lock.yml\u003c/code\u003e workflow that GitHub Actions executes. \u003ca href=\"https://github.github.com/gh-aw/introduction/how-they-work/\"\u003eLearn how GitHub Agentic Workflows works\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eSecurity and permissions\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-security-and-permissions\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Security and permissions\" href=\"#security-and-permissions\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSecurity, permissions, and controlled writes are core design concerns. The supported agent-job path defaults to read-only GitHub access and sandboxed execution. Safe outputs buffer configured writes, validate them, and apply them in separate jobs with scoped permissions. These controls are configurable, so workflow authors must review permissions, tools, network access, and generated files before deployment. \u003ca href=\"https://github.github.com/gh-aw/introduction/architecture/\"\u003eLearn how GitHub Agentic Workflows handles security and permissions\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eUsing agentic workflows in your repository requires careful attention to security considerations and careful human supervision, and even then things can still go wrong. Use it with caution, and at your own risk.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" GitHub Agentic Workflows","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003eAgent quick links\u003c/summary\u003e","","Hello fellow agent! Welcome to GitHub Agentic Workflows = Actions + Agent + Safety.","Here are some pointers to get you started in using this tool.","","- Create a new workflow: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/create.md","- Install: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/install.md","- Package a workflow repository: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/package.md","- Reference: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/.github/aw/github-agentic-workflows.md","","\u003c/details\u003e","","GitHub Agentic Workflows (`gh-aw`) lets developers define AI-powered repository automation in Markdown with YAML frontmatter and run AI agents securely through GitHub Actions. The `gh-aw` GitHub CLI extension compiles each agentic workflow into a standard GitHub Actions workflow.","","Use conventional GitHub Actions for deterministic builds, tests, linting, deployments, and reproducible scripts. Add an agentic workflow when a task needs reasoning or interpretation, such as issue triage, pull-request review, CI failure investigation, documentation maintenance, dependency analysis, or repository reporting. GitHub Agentic Workflows complements existing CI/CD; it does not replace it.","","Built-in AI engines include GitHub Copilot, Claude Code, OpenAI Codex, Google Gemini, and Pi. Agent jobs are read-only and sandboxed by default, and configured GitHub writes are normally applied through validated `safe-outputs` jobs with scoped permissions.","","","## Contents","","- [Quick Start](#quick-start)","- [How Agentic Workflows work](#how-github-agentic-workflows-works)","- [Security and permissions](#security-and-permissions)","- [Documentation](#documentation)","- [Contributing](#contributing)","- [Community Contributions](#-community-contributions)","- [Related Projects](#related-projects)","- [Workshop](#workshop)","","\u003e [!NOTE]","\u003e A [security vulnerability](https://github.com/github/gh-aw/security/advisories/GHSA-8h78-hpm7-29gg) was discovered in versions `\u003e= 0.83.3, \u003c 0.85.4` and, as a result, those releases were retired as a pre-emptive measure.","","## Quick Start","","Install the GitHub CLI extension:","","```bash","gh extension install github/gh-aw","```","","Then follow the [GitHub Agentic Workflows quickstart](https://github.github.com/gh-aw/setup/quick-start/) to select an AI engine, add a sample workflow, and run it through GitHub Actions.","","## How Agentic Workflows work","","An agentic workflow has two parts: YAML frontmatter configures triggers, permissions, tools, and the AI engine; the Markdown body tells the AI agent what to accomplish. The `gh aw compile` command validates this source and generates the `.lock.yml` workflow that GitHub Actions executes. [Learn how GitHub Agentic Workflows works](https://github.github.com/gh-aw/introduction/how-they-work/).","","## Security and permissions","","Security, permissions, and controlled writes are core design concerns. The supported agent-job path defaults to read-only GitHub access and sandboxed execution. Safe outputs buffer configured writes, validate them, and apply them in separate jobs with scoped permissions. These controls are configurable, so workflow authors must review permissions, tools, network access, and generated files before deployment. [Learn how GitHub Agentic Workflows handles security and permissions](https://github.github.com/gh-aw/introduction/architecture/).","","Using agentic workflows in your repository requires careful attention to security considerations and careful human supervision, and even then things can still go wrong. Use it with caution, and at your own risk.","","## Documentation","","Use the [GitHub Agentic Workflows documentation](https://github.github.com/gh-aw/) for these paths:","","- [Create an agentic workflow](https://github.github.com/gh-aw/setup/creating-workflows/)","- [Choose and authenticate an AI engine](https://github.github.com/gh-aw/reference/engines/)","- [Browse GitHub Agentic Workflows examples by task](https://github.github.com/gh-aw/examples/)","- [Review the security architecture](https://github.github.com/gh-aw/introduction/architecture/)","- [Read the GitHub Agentic Workflows FAQ](https://github.github.com/gh-aw/reference/faq/)","","For AI agents and retrieval tools, use the published [agent prompt index](https://github.github.com/gh-aw/llms.txt), [full prompt corpus](https://github.github.com/gh-aw/llms-full.txt), and [AI-readable project summary](https://github.github.com/gh-aw/ai/summary.json).","","## Contributing","","For development setup and contribution guidelines, see [CONTRIBUTING.md](CONTRIBUTING.md).","","### Custom Go linters","","To build and test repository custom linters:","","- `go test ./pkg/linters/\u003clinter-name\u003e/...`","- `go build ./cmd/linters`","- `make golint-custom`","","`make golint-custom` builds `cmd/linters` and runs the custom analyzers against `./cmd/...` and `./pkg/...`.","","","","## 🌍 Community Contributions","","\u003csup\u003eCommunity members whose issues were resolved — updated automatically.\u003c/sup\u003e","","[@a-sjogren-accenture <details>
<summary>Agent quick links</summary>
<p dir="auto">Hello fellow agent! Welcome to GitHub Agentic Workflows = Actions + Agent + Safety.
Here are some pointers to get you started in using this tool.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Create a new workflow: <a href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/create.md" rel="nofollow">https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/create.md</a></li>
<li>Install: <a href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/install.md" rel="nofollow">https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/install.md</a></li>
<li>Package a workflow repository: <a href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/package.md" rel="nofollow">https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/package.md</a></li>
<li>Reference: <a href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/.github/aw/github-agentic-workflows.md" rel="nofollow">https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/.github/aw/github-agentic-workflows.md</a></li>
</ul>
</details>
<p dir="auto">GitHub Agentic Workflows (<code>gh-aw</code>) lets developers define AI-powered repository automation in Markdown with YAML frontmatter and run AI agents securely through GitHub Actions. The <code>gh-aw</code> GitHub CLI extension compiles each agentic workflow into a standard GitHub Actions workflow.</p>
<p dir="auto">Use conventional GitHub Actions for deterministic builds, tests, linting, deployments, and reproducible scripts. Add an agentic workflow when a task needs reasoning or interpretation, such as issue triage, pull-request review, CI failure investigation, documentation maintenance, dependency analysis, or repository reporting. GitHub Agentic Workflows complements existing CI/CD; it does not replace it.</p>
<p dir="auto">Built-in AI engines include GitHub Copilot, Claude Code, OpenAI Codex, Google Gemini, and Pi. Agent jobs are read-only and sandboxed by default, and configured GitHub writes are normally applied through validated <code>safe-outputs</code> jobs with scoped permissions.</p>

<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-note" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-info mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M0 8a8 8 0 1 1 16 0A8 8 0 0 1 0 8Zm8-6.5a6.5 6.5 0 1 0 0 13 6.5 6.5 0 0 0 0-13ZM6.5 7.75A.75.75 0 0 1 7.25 7h1a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v2.75h.25a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5h.25v-2h-.25a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75ZM8 6a1 1 0 1 1 0-2 1 1 0 0 1 0 2Z"></path></svg>Note</p><p dir="auto">A <a href="https://github.com/github/gh-aw/security/advisories/GHSA-8h78-hpm7-29gg">security vulnerability</a> was discovered in versions <code>&gt;= 0.83.3, &lt; 0.85.4</code> and, as a result, those releases were retired as a pre-emptive measure.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick Start</h2><a id="user-content-quick-start" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick Start" href="#quick-start"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Install the GitHub CLI extension:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="gh extension install github/gh-aw"><pre>gh extension install github/gh-aw</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Then follow the <a href="https://github.github.com/gh-aw/setup/quick-start/">GitHub Agentic Workflows quickstart</a> to select an AI engine, add a sample workflow, and run it through GitHub Actions.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">How Agentic Workflows work</h2><a id="user-content-how-agentic-workflows-work" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: How Agentic Workflows work" href="#how-agentic-workflows-work"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">An agentic workflow has two parts: YAML frontmatter configures triggers, permissions, tools, and the AI engine; the Markdown body tells the AI agent what to accomplish. The <code>gh aw compile</code> command validates this source and generates the <code>.lock.yml</code> workflow that GitHub Actions executes. <a href="https://github.github.com/gh-aw/introduction/how-they-work/">Learn how GitHub Agentic Workflows works</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Security and permissions</h2><a id="user-content-security-and-permissions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Security and permissions" href="#security-and-permissions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Security, permissions, and controlled writes are core design concerns. The supported agent-job path defaults to read-only GitHub access and sandboxed execution. Safe outputs buffer configured writes, validate them, and apply them in separate jobs with scoped permissions. These controls are configurable, so workflow authors must review permissions, tools, network access, and generated files before deployment. <a href="https://github.github.com/gh-aw/introduction/architecture/">Learn how GitHub Agentic Workflows handles security and permissions</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Using agentic workflows in your repository requires careful attention to security considerations and careful human supervision, and even then things can still go wrong. Use it with caution, and at your own risk.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Documentation</h2><a id="user-content-documentation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Documentation" href="#documentation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Use the <a href="https://github.github.com/gh-aw/">GitHub Agentic Workflows documentation</a> for these paths:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://github.github.com/gh-aw/setup/creating-workflows/">Create an agentic workflow</a></li>
<li><a href="https://github.github.com/gh-aw/reference/engines/">Choose and authenticate an AI engine</a></li>
<li><a href="https://github.github.com/gh-aw/examples/">Browse GitHub Agentic Workflows examples by task</a></li>
<li><a href="https://github.github.com/gh-aw/introduction/architecture/">Review the security architecture</a></li>
<li><a href="https://github.github.com/gh-aw/reference/faq/">Read the GitHub Agentic Workflows FAQ</a></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">For AI agents and retrieval tools, use the published <a href="https://github.github.com/gh-aw/llms.txt">agent prompt index</a>, <a href="https://github.github.com/gh-aw/llms-full.txt">full prompt corpus</a>, and <a href="https://github.github.com/gh-aw/ai/summary.json">AI-readable project summary</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Contributing</h2><a id="user-content-contributing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Contributing" href="#contributing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">For development setup and contribution guidelines, see <a href="/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md">CONTRIBUTING.md</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Custom Go linters</h3><a id="user-content-custom-go-linters" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Custom Go linters" href="#custom-go-linters"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To build and test repository custom linters:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>go test ./pkg/linters/&lt;linter-name&gt;/...</code></li>
<li><code>go build ./cmd/linters</code></li>
<li><code>make golint-custom</code></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><code>make golint-custom</code> builds <code>cmd/linters</code> and runs the custom analyzers against <code>./cmd/...</code> and <code>./pkg/...</code>.</p>
